# The Financing, Management and Monitoring of Direct Payments to Farmers (Amendment) Regulations 2020

> **Warning: changes not yet applied to this text (1).** Text as at 2024-01-01. See https://www.legislation.gov.uk/changes/affected/uksi/2020/90
>
> Extent: England, Wales, Scotland, Northern Ireland.

*Extent: E+W+S+N.I.*

2020 No. 90

Agriculture

The Financing, Management and Monitoring of Direct Payments to Farmers (Amendment) Regulations 2020 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

Approved by both Houses of Parliament *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

Made *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

30th January 2020 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

Laid before Parliament *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

31st January 2020 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

Coming into force in accordance with regulation 1 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

The Secretary of State makes these Regulations in exercise of the powers conferred by sections 3(1), (7) and (9) of the Direct Payments to Farmers (Legislative Continuity) Act 2020 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] [^c24096691], and in accordance with paragraph 1(2) of Schedule 5 to the European Union (Withdrawal Agreement) Act 2020 *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] [^c24096701]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

## PART 1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Introductory (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

### 1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Citation, commencement, application and extent (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-0341b9f0145b37526440b575c544c65d][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] These Regulations may be cited as the Financing, Management and Monitoring of Direct Payments to Farmers (Amendment) Regulations 2020. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] These Regulations come into force on exit day and paragraph 1(1) of Schedule 5 to the European Union (Withdrawal Agreement) Act 2020 does not apply. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] Subject to paragraph 4, these Regulations extend to the United Kingdom. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] Regulation 16 extends to England and Wales and applies in England only. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

## PART 2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Amendment of Regulation (EU) 1306/2013 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

### 2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Amendment of Regulation (EU) 1306/2013 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-6135e9b2a458815bb64a38bfe4270a36][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 of the European Parliament and of the Council on the financing, management and monitoring of the common agricultural policy in so far as it relates to the Common Agricultural Policy (“CAP”) direct payment schemes is amended in accordance with regulations 3 to 11. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

### 3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Amendment of Title 1 (Scope and Definitions) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-58eaf3126d68607cacbd3809b27fa630][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] In Article 1— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in point (a), for the words from “under” to the end substitute *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] “ for direct payments ” *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
- (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in point (c), for “Member States” substitute *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] “ relevant authority ” *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
- (c (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] omit point (e). *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] In Article 2— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

- (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in paragraph 1— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
  - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] for point (f), substitute— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

    > - (f (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**sectoral agricultural legislation *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**” means, insofar as it relates to direct payments: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >   - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] any applicable acts adopted within the framework of the common agricultural policy on the basis of Article 43 TFEU; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >   - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] any delegated or implementing acts adopted on the basis of acts referred to in point (i); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >   - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] regulations made in the exercise of powers under any act referred to in point (i); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

    ; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
  - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] for point (g), substitute— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

    > - (g (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**irregularity *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**” means any infringement of a provision of sectoral agricultural legislation resulting from an act or omission by an economic operator, where the infringement results, or would result, in a reduction or loss of public revenue or an unjustified item of expenditure; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

    ; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
  - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] after point (g), insert— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

    > - (h (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**constituent nation *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**” means England, Wales, Scotland or Northern Ireland, as the case may be; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    > - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**direct payment support *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**” means financial support granted under any of the support schemes listed in Annex I to Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    > - (j (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**economic operator *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**” means any person or public entity or group of such persons or entities, including any temporary association of undertakings, which offers the execution of works or a work, the supply of products or the provision of services on the market; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    > - (k (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**relevant authority *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**” means: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >   - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in England, the Secretary of State; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >   - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in Wales, the Welsh Ministers; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >   - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in Scotland, the Scottish Ministers; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >   - (iv (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in Northern Ireland, the Department of Agriculture, Environment and Rural Affairs; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    > - (l (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**relevant accounting officer *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**” means: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >   - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in England, the accounting officer for the Department for Environment, Food and Rural Affairs; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >   - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in Wales, the Permanent Secretary of the Welsh Government; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >   - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in Scotland, the Permanent Secretary of the Scottish Government; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >   - (iv (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in Northern Ireland, the Permanent Secretary of the Department of Agriculture, Environment and Rural Affairs;”. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    > - (m (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] “ *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**appropriate authority *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**” means: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >   - (i (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] subject to point (ii), the relevant authority for the constituent nation in which the regulations apply; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >   - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] the Secretary of State: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >     - (aa (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in relation to Scotland, if consent is given by the Scottish Ministers; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
    >     - (bb (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in relation to Northern Ireland, if consent is given by the Department of Agriculture, Environment and Rural Affairs. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

    . *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
  - (iv (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] in paragraph 2, for “the CAP” substitute *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] “ expenditure on direct payments and for the purposes of sectoral agricultural legislation ” *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

### 4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Amendment of Title 2 (General Provisions on Agricultural Funds) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-9fba147a2a492f0e3956289ba318db5b][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] Omit Articles 3, 4 and 6. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

(2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] For Article 7 substitute— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

> **Article 7 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Designation of paying agencies and coordinated action by relevant authorities (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>
> **1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>
> Paying agencies shall be departments or bodies designated by the relevant authority, which have an administrative organisation and a system of internal control that provide sufficient guarantees that payments are legal and regular, and properly accounted for. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
>
> **2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>
> The number of paying agencies shall be restricted to no more than one per constituent nation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
>
> **3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>
> Paying agencies shall be responsible for the management and control of expenditure on direct payments. With the exception of payment, the carrying out of those tasks may be delegated. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
>
> **4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>
> The relevant authorities shall have joint responsibility for the following tasks: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
>
> - (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] to take or coordinate, as the case may be, actions with a view to resolving deficiencies of a common nature; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
> - (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] to promote and, where possible, ensure harmonised application of sectoral agricultural legislation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

(3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] Omit Articles 8 to 10. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

(4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] In Article 11, omit “for in Union law”. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

### 5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note). Amendment of Title 3 (Farm Advisory System) (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-a66102cfdea43b52faa414fb377b0bca][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

(1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] For Articles 12 to 15 substitute— *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

> **Article 12 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Farm Advisory System (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>
> The relevant authority must have in place a system for advising beneficiaries on land management and farm management (“farm advisory system”). That farm advisory system shall be operated by designated public bodies and/or selected private bodies. That system shall cover the agricultural practices beneficial for the climate and the environment laid down in Chapter 3 of Title III of Regulation (EU) No 1307/2013 and the maintenance of the agricultural area as referred to in point (c) of Article 4(1) of that Regulation. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

>
> **1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>

> **Article 115 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Regulations: general (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>

>
> Regulations made by the Secretary of State or the Welsh Ministers under this Regulation are to be made by statutory instrument. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
>

>

>
> **3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>

>
> **4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>

>
> - (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] contain consequential, incidental, supplementary, transitional or saving provision (including provision amending, repealing or revoking enactments); *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
> - (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] make different provision for different purposes. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
>
> **Article 115A (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Regulations: the Secretary of State (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>
> **1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>
> Except as specified in paragraphs 2 to 6, a statutory instrument containing regulations made by the Secretary of State under this Regulation is subject to annulment in pursuance of a resolution of either House of Parliament. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
>
> **2 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>
> A statutory instrument containing regulations made by the Secretary of State under Article 26(1) may not be made unless a draft of the instrument has been laid before, and approved by a resolution of, each House of Parliament. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
>
> **3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>
> A statutory instrument containing regulations made by the Secretary of State under Article 75(3) must be laid before Parliament after being made. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
>
> **4 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>
> Regulations made by the Secretary of State under Article 75(3) cease to have effect at the end of the period of 28 days beginning with the day on which the instrument is made unless, during that period, the instrument is approved by resolution of each House of Parliament. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
>
> **5 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>
> In calculating the period of 28 days for the purposes of paragraph 4, no account is to be taken of any time during which: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
>
> - (a (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] Parliament is dissolved or prorogued, or *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
> - (b (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] either House of Parliament is adjourned for more than 4 days. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
>
> **6 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
>
> If regulations cease to have effect as a result of paragraph 4, that does not affect the validity of anything previously done under those regulations or prevent the making of new regulations. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
>
> **Article 115B (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note) — Regulations: the Welsh Ministers (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note)[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f][^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**

  > **1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
  >
  > In accordance with Article 7(4) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 the relevant authorities will act jointly on the following: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
  >

  > **1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
  >
  > The relevant authorities may decide to reduce the minimum level of on-the-spot checks in accordance with Article 59(5) of Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013. For the reduced control rate to apply: *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

  > - (ii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] the opinion must validate both that the internal control system is functioning properly and that the error rate for the population concerned was below the materiality threshold of 2.0%; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
  > - (iii (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note))[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f] the opinion must cover at least the two consecutive financial years preceding the year in which the reduced rate is intended to apply. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

  > **1 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**

  > **3 (Extent: E) (affected by repeal, see note).[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]**
  >
  > For the purposes of Article 33, between 20% and 25% of the minimum number of beneficiaries to be subject to on-the-spot checks shall be selected randomly. The remaining number of beneficiaries to be subject to on-the-spot checks shall be selected on the basis of a risk analysis. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]
  >
  > For the purposes of Article 33, the random part of the sample may also include either the beneficiaries already randomly selected in accordance with points (a), (b) and (c) of paragraph 2 or the additional beneficiaries selected randomly in accordance with the second subparagraph of Article 26(4) or both. The number of such beneficiaries in the control sample shall not go beyond their proportion in the control population. *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

  ; *(Extent: E)* *(affected by repeal, see note)*[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

### 16. Amendments to the Common Agricultural Policy (Control and Enforcement, Cross-Compliance, Scrutiny of Transactions and Appeals) Regulations 2014[^c-49569664563c97783bf3e7151cc60d1f]

*Extent: E+W*

. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .

George Eustice

Minister of State

Department for Environment, Food and Rural Affairs

*(This note is not part of the Regulations)*

These Regulations are made in exercise of the powers conferred by section 3(1), (7) and (9) of the Direct Payments to Farmers (Legislative Continuity) Act (“**the Act**”) in order to enable retained EU law governing the CAP direct payment schemes for claim year 2020 to operate effectively and to address other deficiencies arising from the UK's withdrawal from the European Union.

Regulations 2 to 11 make amendments to Regulation (EU) No 1306/2013 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 17 December 2013 on the financing, management and monitoring of the common agricultural policy (“**the Horizontal Regulation**”), in so far as that Regulation relates to the CAP direct payment schemes for claim year 2020.

Regulation 12 makes amendments to Commission Delegated Regulation (EU) No 907/2014 of 11 March 2014 supplementing the Horizontal Regulation with regard to paying agencies and other bodies, financial management, clearance of accounts, securities and use of euro, in so far as that Regulation relates to the CAP direct payment schemes for claim year 2020.

Regulation 13 makes amendments to Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) No 908/2014 of 6 August 2014 laying down rules for the application of the Horizontal Regulation with regard to paying agencies and rules on checks, securities and transparency, in so far as that Regulation relates to the CAP direct payment schemes for claim year 2020.

Regulation 14 makes amendments to Commission Implementing Regulation (EU) No 809/2014 of 17 July 2014 laying down rules for the application of the administration and control system, rural development measures and cross compliance, in so far as that Regulation relates to the CAP direct payment schemes for claim year 2020.

Regulation 15 makes amendments to Commission Delegated Regulation (EU) No 640/2014 of 11 March 2014 supplementing the Horizontal Regulation with regard to the integrated administration and control system and conditions for refusal or withdrawal of payments and administrative penalties applicable to direct payments, rural development support and cross compliance, in so far as that Regulation relates to the CAP direct payment schemes for claim year 2020.

Regulation 16 makes amendments to The Common Agricultural Policy (Control and Enforcement, Cross Compliance, Scrutiny of Transactions and Appeals) Regulations S.I. 2014/3263, which implements EU obligations in relation to the common agricultural policy under the powers in section 2(2) of the European Community Act. These amendments only apply to England.

Section 3(7) of the Act clarifies that provisions made by regulations under the powers in the Act may apply in relation to matters arising in relation to the CAP direct payment schemes for the whole of the 2020 claim year.

Regulation 1(2) disapplies paragraph 1(1) of Schedule 5 to the European Union (Withdrawal Agreement) Act. This ensures that the regulations come into force on exit day.

An impact assessment has not been produced for this instrument as no, or no significant, impact on the private or voluntary sector is foreseen.
